පාර්ලිදේන්තුෙ

ඇතුද.න අළි සාකච්ඡා කරමින් තිදෙනො. අදේ සංෙර්ලධයන ෙයාපෘතිෙලට ණය ගන්න අතරතුදර්ලදීම 2025 ෙර්ල දේදී ිරිත්පත් කරන ල 2026 සඳහා වූ අය ෙැදේදී ගරු ජනාධිපතිතුමා ිරිත්පත් ක.ා, රුළියලීම බිියන 5.6ක සංෙර්ලධයනයක ිලකක කර ගත් ණය සහ දපාීම ආධයාර ලො ද න ෙැ සටහනක. විදශන දයන්ම කුද්‍ර, සුළු හා මධයය පත්මාණ කර්ලමාන්තකරුෙන් ිලකක කරදගන ආර්ලිකය ප්‍රසාරණය කිරීම සඳහා ණය සැලසුේ ිරිත්පත් කරලා තිබුණා. ින් එකක තමයි, ආසියානු සංෙර්ලධයන ෙැකුෙ මිනන් ප්‍රතිපත්තිමය පා කෙ ලො ද න ද ාලර්ල මිියන 100ක ණය. ඒ ොදේම කුද්‍ර, සුළු හා මධයය පත්මාණ කර්ලමාන්ත හා කෘි කර්ලමාන්තය පා ක කර ගත් ණය ලො දීමට රුළියලීම ද ාලර්ල මිියන 100කුත් දෙන් කර තිබුණා. කුද්‍ර, සුළු හා මධයය පත්මාණ කර්ලමාන්ත සඳහා ණය ලො දීදේදී ඒ ෙයෙසායකයින්ට සහන ලො දීමට අළි ෙැංකු පේධයතිය භාවිත කරනො. ඒ ෙැංකු පේධයතිය තු. රාජය සහ දපෞේගික ෙැංකු තිදෙනො. සාමානයදයන් අළි රාජය ෙැංකු විශනොස කරනො, රාජය ෙයෙසායයන් විධියට. නුදත් 2024 අංක 33 රන රාජය ණය ක.මනාකරණ පනතට අනුෙ ැන් ඒක අර්ලථ කථනය ෙනො, රාජය ෙැංකුෙක රාජය ෙයෙසාය යටතට ෙැදටන්දන් නැහැ කියලා. ඒ නිසා රාජය ෙැංකුෙකට දහෝ දපෞේගික ෙැංකුෙකට දේ ණය අ ා. ිලීමලුේකරුට ලො දීදේ ක්‍රියාපටිපාටියට යන්න අෙශය නේ, පාර්ලිදේන්තුද අෙසරය ලො ගන්න සිේධය ෙනො. ඒ අනුෙ රාජය ෙැංකු 5ක - ලංකා ෙැංකුෙ, මහජන ෙැංකුෙ, ජාතික ිතිත්කිරීදේ ෙැංකුෙ සහ තෙත් ෙැංකු ද කක - සහ දපෞේගික අංශදේ ෙැංකු හරහා දේ රුළියලීම බිියන 9,950ක ණය ප්‍රමාණය අ ා. සංෙර්ලධයන කටයුත්තට එබඳයට ලො ද න්දන් දකදසන කියලා අළි කලීමපනා කරනො. අළි විශනොස කරනො, ද සැේෙර්ල 31ෙැනි ාට කින් එය ිෙර කරන්න ඉළුෙන් දෙයි කියලා. ඒ සඳහා පාර්ලිදේන්තුද අෙසරය ලො ගැනීදේ පරමාර්ලථදයන් තමයි දමය ිරිත්පත් කරන්දන්.

ගරු කථානායකතුමනි, අ දේ ිරිත්පත් කර තිදෙන දරණවලාසි ද දකත්, නිදයෝගදේත්, රාජය ණය ක.මනාකරණ පනත යටදත් වූ දයෝජනාද ත් ප්‍රධයානම අරුදණ ෙන්දන්, අළි දේ අවුරුදු ද ක ඇතු.තදී අත්පත් කරගනිමින් ආ, පෙත්ොදගන ආ මූලය විනය නිසියාකාරයට දපන්වීමත්, ො දර අංශය ශකතිමත් කිරීමට සංිත දගා නඟා ගත්දත් දකාදහාම , ඒ සනථාෙරත්ෙය එදලසින්ම රැක ගනිමින්, ඒ සනථාෙරත්ෙදයන් ඔුෙට දගනයෑමත්, ඊ.ඟට අළි .ඟා කර ගත යුතු අතයෙශයම ආර්ලික ෙර්ලධයන ද ග අත්පත් කරගැනීදේ ෙැ ළිබඳදෙ.ට ායක ෙන්න අෙශය අනුමැතිය ලො ගැනීමත් කියන ඒො. ඒ සඳහා සියලුද නාදේ සහදයෝගය අදේක ා කරමින් මදේ කථාෙ අෙසන් කරනො.

දොදහාම සනතුතියි. ප්‍රශ්නය සභාිමමුඛ කතන ල ජ. வினா எடுத்தியம்பப்சபற்றது. Question proposed. ගරු කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Speaker) The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva. You have 14 minutes. ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) Sir, before I start, I have a point of Order to raise.

ගරු කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Speaker) What is your point of Order? ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) ගරු ඇමතිතුමනි, "Participating Financial Institutions" ද රජදේ ෙැංකු එකකෙත් නැහැ දන්. There are the HNB, the Seylan Bank, the Sampath Bank, the Commercial Bank, the DFCC Bank, the Pan Asia Bank, the NDB Bank, the Union Bank, the Sanasa Bank, the NTB and the Cargills Bank. ඔෙතුමා කි ො, රජදේ ෙැංකු 5ක තිදෙනො කියලා.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) අනිල් ගයන්තත මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) அனில் ஜயந்த) (The Hon. (Dr.) Anil Jayantha) ඔෙතුමා කි ද දපෞේගික ෙැංකු ළිබඳෙඳෙ. ඒ ෙැ සටහන් ද ක සඳහා අ ා. ෙන අදනක ෙැංකු තුන තමයි, ලංකා ෙැංකුෙ, මහජන ෙැංකුෙ සහ ප්‍රාදේයය සංෙර්ලධයන ෙැංකුෙ. ඊ.ඟට HDFCC සහ SMIB. ඔෙතුමා කියන කාරණය හත්. අ ිරිත්පත් කරන SME දකාටසට අතිදර්ලකෙ ඇතු.ත් කරන්දන් දපෞේගික ෙැංකු. නුදත් ඊ.ඟට එන agricultural value chain එකට දේ රාජය ෙැංකු 5 සේෙන්ධය ෙනො.

ගරු කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Speaker) Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva, you have 14 minutes. You can start now.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) ගරු කථානායකතුමනි, දේක පැහැරිි කර ගැනීමක විතරයි. මට තිදෙන විනාි 14 අු කරන්න එපා.

ගරු කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Speaker) It is not the Debate.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) It is not the Debate, but I need a clarification.

ගරු කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Speaker) Order, please! The Hon. Deputy Speaker will now take the Chair.

අනුරුව ගරු කථානායකුමා මූලාසනමයන්ත ඉව් අමයන්ත නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා [ගරු (වවදය) රිස්වි සාි මහතා මූලාසනාරූඪ විය.

அதன்பிறகு, மாண்புமிகு ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள் அக்கிராெனத்தினின்று அகைரவ, பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள் [மாண்புமிகு (மவத்தியா்) ாிஸ்வி ொலி] தமைமம வகித்தார்கள்.

(Dr.) Anil Jayantha) ගරු නිදයෝජය කථානායකතුමනි, ප.ුදදෙන් ිරිත්පත් කරන අෙසනථාද දී දපෞේගික ෙැංකු පමණක ඇතු.ත් කර දේක ිරිත්පත් කර තිබුදණ්ඩ රාජය ෙැංකු රාජය ෙයෙසායක කියන understanding එක මත. නුදත්, රාජය ණය ක.මනාකරණ පනත යටදත් ඒ සඳහාත් පාර්ලිදේන්තුද අෙසරය ලො ගන්න ඕනෑ. ඒ අනුෙ තමයි, දේ සියලීමලම ඇතු.ත් කර නිෙැරරි කරන ල දලීමඛ්‍නය ිරිත්පත් කර තිදෙන්දන්.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) ඒ කියන්දන්, නිෙැරරි කර ඉ දලීමඛ්‍නය තෙම ිරිත්පත් දෙලා නැහැ, කෙ ා දහෝ ඒක ිරිත්පත් කරන්න දෙයි.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva, you have 14 minutes and your time starts now.

[12.08 p.m.] ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) Thank you, Hon. Deputy Speaker.

Today, I would like to speak on six matters with relevance to what was proposed today. The first one is, the debt ratio is falling. But, the point is that it is a story which spans longer than one year. Sir, the public debt-to-GDP ratio at the end of 2025 was 98 per cent and it is expected to go down to about 93 per cent, which is a positive development, and we must acknowledge it. But, one thing we need to understand, Sir, is that most of the reduction came during 2022-2024, during which period the ratio fell from 121 per cent to 101 per cent. So, the result of hard decisions on revenue, spending and restructuring was taken before this Government came to office, and we must appreciate all of that. The higher than expected primary surplus, which these people have been singing about loudly, has helped to push the ratio down faster than what the IMF forecasted. Sir, we should be honest about why it happened. Part of that surplus comes from spending less than what was budgeted, including capital spending. So, in 2025, only 76 per cent of Capital Expenditure - CapEx - was spent and so far, from January to August this year, only 26 per cent of it has been spent. I heard the President and others saying, “Look, we are going to allocate so much on Capital Expenditure.” It has to be done, but Capital Expenditure also has to be spent. Underspending is not the same thing as fiscal reform and it costs us growth. He said that they are going to spend more than 4 per cent on Capital Expenditure. That is what you need to do, but you have to actually spend it. There is no point in saying you are going to spend it, if you do not spend it. Also, the higher than expected revenue in 2025 came due to the vehicle pent-up demand. Actually 63 per cent or Rs. 900 billion out of the Rs. 1.4 trillion revenue increase in 2025 was vehicle import-related. So, that is not sustainable beyond this year. As Fitch Ratings pointed out in its upgrade, Sri Lanka’s external debt and debt in total is still very high. Interest alone is expected to take about 41 per cent of Government revenue this year. External repayments rise after 2028 towards about US Dollars 4 billion a year by 2030, which leaves the country, I quote, “vulnerable to external shocks”. So, that is the first point I want to make, that the debt ratio is falling, it is a story which spans longer than a year and is based fundamentally on the reforms, the hard reforms that were taken, and they have to continue to do so. The Hon. Minister just mentioned that the IMF said that they will give the money if, only "if", the Budget is presented based on the agreement with the IMF. So, when various politicians on the other side of the aisle say in public rallies, “Look, we are getting rid of the IMF on 17th of March, and thereafter, it is our baby”, it is not true because you are admitting with the Budget that it would be in line with the IMF for the entirety of 2027.

Sir, the second point I want to make is, repayments remain heavy, but new financing is thin. Repayments are still high both on domestic and foreign sides. The gross financing need has to be covered every year, not just in

85 86

පාර්ලිදේන්තුෙ

one good year. Now, the trend is worrying. For instance, multilateral and bilateral financing has been slow. Listen to me carefully. Leaving the IMF aside, disbursements from other multilateral institutions have been low, and bilateral financing has nearly dried up. In the first half of 2026, multilateral lenders other than the IMF disbursed only about US Dollars 177 million: World Bank, US Dollars 94 million; ADB, US Dollars 52 million; AIIB, US Dollars 23 million and others, US Dollars 8 million. Sir, bilateral lenders disbursed just US Dollars 24 million: Japan, US Dollars 14 million; India, US Dollars 8 million and Saudi Arabia, US Dollars 3 million. By comparison, the IMF disbursed about US Dollars 690 million. So, six months of near-zero bilateral inflows is not a financing strategy. Even if we take today’s Resolution under the Public Debt Management Act, Sir, which relates to the US Dollars 200 million from the ADB, for this year the Government is expected to spend US Dollars 45 million and it is already provided for in the Budget. The Hon. Minister has not even included State banks in the Resolution brought before this House today. So, this is inefficiency. Now, already ten months have gone by and how are you going to spend that? So, these are issues I am bringing to your attention, Sir.

The other matter is this. We saw in the news that the Ministry of Finance, Planning and Economic Development had signed an agreement with the ADB to the tune of something like US Dollars 57.4 million for rooftop solar and that, too, for Virtual Net Metering, meaning that you virtually aggregate the electricity generated through rooftop solar and then, sell it to the grid. That policy, we do not know. In Sinhala, you call this "හතර බි දත් කථාෙක". The Government is saying, “No more Net Metering; no more Net Accounting” and yet, after saying that, you have signed an agreement with the ADB for US Dollars 57 million to promote rooftop solar! You tell me, Sir, is that not a complete oxymoron? It is an oxymoron because you cannot go in a particular direction and also in the opposite direction at the same time; then, you will be at the same place. So, you have to have a clear policy. One part of the Government cannot say, “We are stopping rooftop solar” while the other part of the Government goes and gets US Dollar 57 million of ADB funding to promote rooftop solar. So, this, absolutely, does not make sense. Therefore, what I am saying is this. Even if you get this money, how are you going to disburse it? Who would want to set up rooftop solar panels anymore? We will talk about that more later because I think this is important not just for people who are in the rooftop solar industry, but for everyone.

The third point is, the Public Debt Management Office, which the Hon. Minister was speaking about, has been very slow in deciding how to get back into the international debt market. The IMF Agreement is basically built on Sri Lanka’s ability to get back into the international debt market.

Now, let me make a valid point, which is my fourth point. If you are interested, you can listen. It is about what are called Macro-Linked Bonds, and I am suggesting that

you buy them back before the market prices us out of the purchase decision. It was the first time in our Committee that both the Government Members and us queried the PDMO whether they are willing to or are, at least, in discussion on a buyback of the MLBs, which are going to be expensive after the test point of the GDP growth. The Government has been criticising the MLBs from the beginning. We agree that there were unfair clauses in that instrument, but it was an unfair bargain which got us out of the default. So, there has to be some compromise. It was not the best deal, but that deal got us out of bankruptcy, it got us out of the default and helped us get to this stable position. Now, the question is this. We are going to have to pay about Rs. 1.2 billion more over the next 10 years because the total payment of the MLBs will go up from US Dollar 6.2 billion to US Dollar 7.4 billion, if the PDMO waits without doing anything. Why is that? It is because we passed the test of the GDP growth. We all know that. If we grow more than what the IMF originally planned, suggested or forecasted, the creditors would not give us the haircut they originally promised; the haircut is going to be cut by a certain percentage. Now the point is, if we are proactive and we could buy back some of those loans that are outstanding - those are financial instruments and the process is called financial engineering, which is why we have set up the PDMO with all these consultants and so on - there is a possibility we could save about US Dollar 300 million to 400 million, according to our own calculations, over the next ten years. If we wait, the market is going to reprice those Bonds, which would no longer give us an incentive to go and buy them back. Therefore, we have to play the market before the market plays us, Hon. Minister. You cannot just wait for things to happen. In 2018, I was the person, as the non-Cabinet Minister, who proposed the piece of legislation called the Active Liability Management Act. I remember that day very well. That day, there was a constitutional coup and we all were sent home. Now, that legislation can be used. That was brought in to manage our liabilities. Now, we have a classic case, Hon. Minister, where, if we are smart enough as a nation, we could manage this liability and save, in my mind, about US Dollars 300 million to US Dollars 400 million. It may not be a huge amount, but it is still bigger than or twice as much the loan we are getting from the ADB for SME development, on which we need to actually spend the money.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva, you have two minutes left.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) Sir, I will get two more minutes from our party.

The fifth point is, our rating upgrade does not guarantee market access. Yes, we have been upgraded from CCC+ to B-, but that does not necessarily mean we have market access. We need to get, at least, to a BB

87 88

[ගරු (ආ්ාර්ලය හර්ල සිලීමො මහතා]

2026 ඔකදතෝෙර්ල 06

rating. Also, you know that only Fitch Ratings has upgraded us and not Moody's or S&P. Therefore, we have to think about the future, what the GDP growth is going to be. Now, they are saying it may be even 3.5 per cent. That is not going to guarantee us an upgrade. This upgrade happened because of what happened previously. That upgrade has to go up from B- to BB or BB+ based on what the Government has in store in going forward.

Then, Sir, my penultimate point is that mixed signals are damaging confidence. The Government’s recent language does not help. The Leader of the House went on television to complain that IMF reviews are too intense. Perhaps the real problem is that the Government lacks people who could push back on IMF officials on technical grounds. Today, with Google Translate, everybody could understand what is being said in Sinhala or Tamil. So, you cannot hide from the IMF and speak in Sinhala; they understand what is being said in Sinhala because of Google Translate or ChatGPT. Then, the President has been promising subsidies and tax cuts at rallies weeks before the Cabinet has decided anything. The markets are listening to these. If you think you can fool the market by speaking in Sinhala, it would not happen. I am speaking in English today because everybody at the IMF and at the international markets could understand what I am saying here. Therefore, do not try to fool the market; be smart and play the market. Without market access, what would happen is, the Government would become more and more dependent on the IMF and the reviews it complains about.

Sir, my last point is, the foreign exchange reserves have recovered, but remain well below the ideal levels of ARA - Assessing Reserve Adequacy - metric of the IMF. We are talking about this US Dollars 13 billion of reserves we need to raise by the end of next year and so on, and it has been adjusted down to US Dollars 11.8 billion. The issue is, within the monies we have in the Central Bank as reserves, there are swaps which cannot be used and also, the real Net International Reserves we have are much lower than what has been publicly announced.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva, you have only one minute left of your additional two minutes.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) Thank you, Sir.

If the Central Bank continues to purchase dollars, then the non-borrowed reserves would go up and that is good. But what has happened is, the Central Bank has, sometimes, not been able to continue to purchase the kind of reserves as they have done in certain months. For instance, in August, the Central Bank bought almost US Dollars 600 million, but in September, it dropped to just US Dollars 65 million.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva, your additional minute has also finished.

ගරු (ආචාර්ලය) හර්ලෂ ද සිල්වා මහතා (மாண்புமிகு (கைாநிதி) ஹர்ஷ த ெில்வா) (The Hon. (Dr.) Harsha de Silva) I will finish my speech in 30 seconds, Sir.

So, the picture I am trying to paint is that you cannot just look at one time period and say, “This is a glorious achievement" and so on. We appreciate the good work of everyone. But, what has to happen is, this needs to be taken forward and sustained and in order to do that, you have to have policy consistency. Unless you have that kind of policy consistency, we would not be able to improve our ratings and get back to the international markets. Thank you, Sir.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) Next, the Hon. Kathiravelu Shanmugam Kugathasan. You have seven minutes.

ගරු කදිතමේලු ෂන්තමුගේ කුගදාසන්ත මහතා (மாண்புமிகு கதிரரவலு ெண்முகம் குகதாென்) (The Hon. Kathiravelu Shanmugam Kugathasan) No, Sir, I have been allotted eight minutes. ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) We have been reducing times allotted right along.

[பி.ப. 12.25]

ගරු කදිතමේලු ෂන්තමුගේ කුගදාසන්ත මහතා (மாண்புமிகு கதிரரவலு ெண்முகம் குகதாென்) (The Hon. Kathiravelu Shanmugam Kugathasan) சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, இன்று நாடாளுமன்றத்தில் முன்மவக்கப்பட்டுள்ள நான்கு ெட்டவாக்கப் சபாருண்மமகளுக்கு ஒப்புதல் அளிப்பது சதாடர்பான எனது கருத்துகமள இந்த அமவயில் எடுத்துமரக்க விமழகின்ரறன்.

இன்று நம்முன் மவக்கப்பட்டுள்ள ெட்டப் சபாருண்மமகள், சபாருண்மிய நிமைத்தன்மமயின் இரண்டு முதன்மமயான தூண்கமளக் மகயாளுகின்றன. ஒன்று, இைங்மகயின் எல்மைகமளத் தாண்டிய மூைதன நகர்வுகமள ரமைாண்மம செய்தல். இரண்டு, இைங்மக நாடானது எவ்வாறு சபாதுக் கடமனப் சபறுகின்றது, பதிவு செய்கின்றது மற்றும் ரெமவ செய்கின்றது என்பமத நிருவகித்தல்.

சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, முதலில் 2493/36 மற்றும் 2493/37ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட அரெிதழ்களில் சவளியிடப்பட்ட, 2017ஆம் ஆண்டின் 12ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட சவளிநாட்டு நாணயமாற்றுச் ெட்டத்தின் 7ஆம் பிாிரவாடு ரெர்த்து வாெிக்கப்படும் இரண்டு ஒழுங்குவிதிகள் குறித்துப்

89 90

පාර්ලිදේන්තුෙ

ரபெ விரும்புகின்ரறன். முதைாவதாக, 2026ஆம் ஆண்டின் 1ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட ஒழுங்குவிதியானது, இைங்மகயில் வெிப்ரபார் சவளிநாடுகளில் ரமற்சகாள்ளும் முதலீடு களுக்கான பங்கு வருவாய் மற்றும் மூைதன வருவாய் என்பவற்மற ஈட்டிய 180 நாட்களுக்குள் இைங்மக சவளிநாட்டு நாணயக் கணக்குகளுக்குத் திரும்ப அனுப்பப்பட ரவண்டுசமனக் கட்டுப்படுத்துகின்றது. இதன்மூைம் சவளிச்செல்லும் மூைதனப் பாய்ச்ெல் இைங்மகயின் உள்நாட்டு வங்கி முமறக்கு ஆதரவளிப்பமதயும் ரதெிய இருப்புகமள வலுப்படுத்துவமதயும் உறுதி செய்கின்றது.

2026ஆம் ஆண்டின் 2ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட ஒழுங்குவிதியானது, சவளிநாடுகளில் வாழும் இைங்மகயரது சவளிநாட்டுப் பணப் பாிமாற்றங்கமளக் மகயாள்கின்றது. சவளிநாடுகளில் வாழும் இைங்மகயர், ெட்ட அடிப்பமடயில் ஈட்டிய உள்நாட்டுச் சொத்துக்கமளப் பாிமாற்றம் செய்ய முயலும்சபாழுது, ெிக்கைான நிருவாகக் சகாள்மககள், தன்னிச்மெயான முடக்கம் மற்றும் அடிக்கடி மாறும் விதிகமள எதிர்சகாண்டனர். இது இைங்மக வங்கி அமமப்பின் மீதான அவர்களது நம்பிக்மகமயச் ெிமதத்து, முமறொராப் பணப்பாிமாற்ற வமைப்பின்னல்கமளப் பயன்படுத்தத் தூண்டியது. 2026ஆம் ஆண்டின் 2ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட ஒழுங்குவிதியானது, நியாயமான, சவளிப்பமடயான மற்றும் விதிகள் ொர்ந்த ஓர் அமமப்மப உருவாக்குகிறது. இந்த அமமப்பானது, திடீர் பணப்புழக்க அதிர்ச்ெியில் இருந்து இைங்மகயின் சவளிநாட்டு நாணய இருப்புகமளப் பாதுகாக்கும் அரதரவமளயில், சவளிநாடுகளில் வாழும் இைங்மகயரது ெட்ட அடிப்பமடயிைான உாிமமகமளயும் மதிக்கிறது.

சகளரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, இந்த ஒழுங்குவிதிகரளாடு, 2493/38ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட அரெிதழில் சவளியிடப்பட்ட சவளிநாட்டு நாணயமாற்றுச் ெட்டத்தின் 22ஆம் பிாிவின்கீழ் பிறப்பிக்கப்பட்ட கட்டமளமயயும் நாம் பாிெீலிக்கிரறாம். பிாிவு 22ஆனது ஒரு ரதமவயான சபருரநாக்கு நிதிப் பாதுகாப்பு ஒழுங்குமுமற அமமப்பாகச் செயற்படுகிறது. இது சவளிநாட்டு நாணயமாற்றுச் ெிக்கல் நிைவும் காைங்களில், நடப்புக் கணக்குப் பாிவர்த்தமன, வர்த்தகத் தீர்வு மற்றும் அந்நிய ரநரடி முதலீட்டு வருவாய் பாிமாற்றங்கமளப் பாதுகாக்கும் அரதரவமளயில், ரதமவயற்ற மூைதன சவளிரயற்றங்கமளக் கட்டுப்படுத்த நிதி அமமச்ெருக்குத் தற்காலிகப் பாதுகாப்பு அதிகாரத்மத வழங்குகிறது.

சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, அடுத்ததாக நான் இன்மறய ஒழுங்குப் பத்திரத்தின் விவர எண் *1ஐ ரநாக்கி எனது கவனத்மதத் திருப்புகிரறன். இது 2024ஆம் ஆண்டின் 33ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட அரசுக்கடன் ரமைாண்மமச் ெட்டத்தின் 16ஆம் பிாிவின் கீழான ெட்ட அடிப்பமடயிைான தீர்மானமாகும். அரசுக்கடன் ரமைாண்மமச் ெட்டத்மத நிமறரவற்றியது இைங்மகயின் சபாது நிதி வரைாற்றில் ஒரு முதன்மமயான கட்டமமப்புச் ெீர்திருத்தமாகும். பை பதிற்றாண்டுகளாக, 1937ஆம் ஆண்டின் பதிவுசெய்யப்பட்ட பங்குகள் மற்றும் பிமணயங்கள் கட்டமளச் ெட்டம், உள்ளூர் திமறரொி உண்டியல்கள் கட்டமளச் ெட்டம் முதலியவற்றின்கீழ், சபாதுக் கடன் ரமைாண்மம செய்யப்பட்டு வந்தது. அரசுக்கடன் ரமைாண்மமச் ெட்டமானது, நிதி அமமச்ெின்கீழ் இயங்கும் சுதந்திரமான அரசுக்கடன் ரமைாண்மம அலுவைகத்துக்குள் கடன் ரமைாண்மமமய

நடுவப்படுத்துவதன்மூைம் இந்த முரண்பாட்மடத் தீர்த்தது. இன்று நம்முன் உள்ள தீர்மானமானது, 2026ஆம் ஆண்டுக்கான சமாத்தக் கடன் வாங்கும் வரம்புக்கு ஒப்புதல் அளிப்பதன்மூைமும் ஆண்டுக்கான கடன் வாங்கல் திட்டத்மத நிறுவுவதன்மூைமும் ெட்டத்தின் பிாிவு 16ஐ நமடமுமறப்படுத்துகிறது.

சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, இந்தத் தீர்மானமானது, அமனத்து அரொங்கக் கடன் வாங்கல்கமளயும் அமமச்ெரமவயால் ஒப்புதல் அளிக்கப்பட்ட நடுத்தரக் காைக் கடன் வழிமுமறரயாடு ரநரடியாக இமணக்கிறது. இந்த 16ஆம் பிாிவின்கீழ் நாடாளுமன்ற முன் ஒப்புதல் இன்றி எந்தரவார் அரசும் கடன் வாங்கரவா அல்ைது உத்தரவாதங்கமள சவளியிடரவா அல்ைது கடன் சகாடுக்கல்வாங்கல்கமள ரமற்சகாள்ளரவா முடியாது. இது ரதெிய பாதீட்டின் மீதான ெட்டவாக்க அதிகாரத்மத மீளமமப்பரதாடு, சபாதுக்கடன் வாங்குதல் கடன் நிமைத்தன்மம அளவுரகாலுக்குள் இருப்பமதயும் உறுதி செய்கின்றது. இருப்பினும், ஒரு சதாமைரநாக்குப் பார்மவயுள்ள நாடாளுமன்றம் என்ற வமகயில், இந்த நடவடிக்மககமளத் திறனாய்வு அடிப்பமடயில் ஆராய ரவண்டும்.

ெந்மதப் பங்ரகற்பாளர்கள், வணிகத் தமைவர்கள் மற்றும் சபாதுமக்களால் எழுப்பப்படும் நமடமுமறக் கவமைகமள நாம் கருத்திற்சகாள்ள ரவண்டும். முதைாவதாக, அரெ படுகடன் முகாமமத்துவச் ெட்டத் தீர்மானம் 2026ஆம் ஆண்டுக்கான சமாத்த நிதித் ரதமவயாக 6,840 பில்லியன் ரூபாமய ஏற்றுக்சகாள்கின்றது. உள்நாட்டுச் ெந்மதக்குள் இந்த அளவு கடன் வாங்கும் அளமவ ஈர்ப்பது, ெிறு மற்றும் நடுத்தர நிறுவனங்களுக்கான கடன் வாய்ப்புகமள முடக்கைாம். அரசு கடன் வாங்குவது, வணிக வங்கிப் பணப்புழக்கத்மத உறிஞ்ெினால், தனியார் துமற ொர்ந்த சபாருண்மிய மீட்மப நம்மால் அமடய முடியாது. இரண்டாவதாக, 2ஆம் எண் சகாண்ட ஒழுங்குவிதியின்கீழ் சவளிநாடுகளில் வாழும் இைங்மகயரது மூைதனப் பாிமாற்றங்கள் சதாடர்பான அவர்களது சவறுப்மப நீக்க ஆவன செய்ய ரவண்டும். மூன்றாவதாக, சபாதுக் கடன் நிருவாகத்மதப் சபாறுத்தவமர, அரசுக் கடன் ரமைாண்மம அலுவைகத்மத நிதி அமமச்சுக்குள் அமமப்பது அரெியல் செல்வாக்குச் செலுத்தும் என்ற அச்ெத்மத உருவாக்குகிறது. இதமன நீக்க ஆவன செய்ய ரவண்டும்.

சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, இந்த அமறகூவல்கமளக் கண்டறிந்து, நாம் நமடமுமறச் ொத்தியமான தீர்வுகமள முன்மவக்கரவண்டிய ரதமவ உள்ளது. முதைாவதாக, தனியார் துமற கடன் வாய்ப்புகள் முடக்கப்படுவமதத் தடுக்க, அரெ படுகடன் முகாமமத்துவச் ெட்டத்தின்கீழ் ஒப்புதல் அளிக்கப்பட்டுள்ளபடி, அரசுக்கடன் ரமைாண்மம அலுவைகமானது ரமைாண்மம நடவடிக்மககமள கடுமமயாகப் பின்பற்ற ரவண்டும். இரண்டாவதாக, சவளிநாட்டு நாணயமாற்று நிருவாகத்மதச் ெீரமமக்க, வணிக வங்கிகள், உள்நாட்டு இமறவாித் திமணக்களம் மற்றும் பதிவாளர் நாயகத் திமணக்களம் ஆகியவற்மற இமணக்கும் ஒருங்கிமணந்த எண்மிய ொிபார்ப்பு இமணயத்தளத்மத அரசு உருவாக்க ரவண்டும். மூன்றாவதாக, சபாதுக் கடன் நடவடிக்மககளின் சுதந்திரத்மத உறுதிசெய்யும் சபாருட்டாக, கடன் வாங்கும் திட்டங்கமள ரமற்பார்மவயிட ஒரு சுதந்திரமான சதாழில்நுட்ப அறிவுமரக் குழுமவ நிறுவ ரவண்டும்.

91 92

[ගරු කරිරද ලු න්ුදගේ කුග ාසන් මහතා]

2026 ඔකදතෝෙර්ල 06

சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, இறுதியாக, சவளிநாட்டு நாணயமாற்றுச் ெட்டத்தின் கீழான பிாிவு 22இன் கட்டமளகள் மாறும் தன்மமயுமடய புறநிமை அளவுரகால்கமள ரநாக்கி நகர ரவண்டும். சதளிவான சபாருளாதாரத் தூண்டுதல்கள் பன்னாட்டு முதலீட்டாளர்களுக்கு உறுதிமய அளிப்பரதாடு, இைங்மக நிமையான திறந்த நிதித் தன்மமக்கு உறுதிபூண்டுள்ளது என்பமதயும் சவளிப்படுத்துகின்றன.

சகௌரவ பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கரள, இன்று நம் முன் உள்ள இந்தச் ெட்டத் ெீர்திருத்தங்கள், சபாருண்மிய நிமைத் தன்மமக்கான கட்டமமப்புச் ெட்டகத்மத வழங்குகின்றன என்பது மறுப்பதற்கில்மை. இருப்பினும், அவற்றின் இறுதி சவற்றியானது, திறமமயான செயற்படுத்தல், நிருவாக சவளிப்பமடத்தன்மம மற்றும் நிறுவனப் சபாறுப்புக்கூறல் ஆகியவற்றிரைரய தங்கியுள்ளது எனக்கூறி, எனது உமரமய நிமறவுசெய்கின்ரறன். நன்றி!

ගරු නිමයෝගය කථානායකුමා (மாண்புமிகு பிரதிச் ெபாநாயகர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Speaker) Order, please! The ongoing Proceedings of this House shall stay suspended till 1.00 p.m. for lunch.

රැස්ීම ඊට අනුකූලව තාවකාිකව අ් හිටුවන ලදින්ත අ.භා. 1.00ට නිමයෝගය කාතක සභා සභාපතිුමියමේ [ගරු මහ්මාි ීතමස්කත මහ් මිය සභාපති් වමයන්ත නැවත පව් වන ල ජ.

அதன்படி, அமர்வு பி.ப. 1.00 மணிவமர இமடநிறுத்தப்பட்டு மீண்டுந் சதாடங்கிற்று. குழுக்களின் பிரதித் தவிொளர் அவர்கள் [மாண்புமிகு (திருமதி) ரஹமாலி வீரரெகர] தமைமம வகித்தார்கள்.

Sitting accordingly suspended till 1.00 p.m. and then resumed, DEPUTY CHAIRPERSON OF COMMITTEES [THE HON. (MRS.) HEMALI WEERASEKARA] in the Chair.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කාතක සභා සභාපතිුමිය (மாண்புமிகு குழுக்களின் பிரதித் தவிொளர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Chairperson of Committees) ගරු ්තුරංග අදුසිංහ නිදයෝජය ඇමතිතුමා. ඔෙතුමාට විනාි 11ක කාලයක ලැ දෙනො.

[1.00 p.m.]

ගරු චුතාග අමේසිාහ මහතා (කර්ලමාන්තත සහ වයවසායක් ව සාවර්ලධන නිමයෝගය අමාතයුමා) (மாண்புமிகு ெதுரங்க அரபெிங்ஹ - மகத்சதாழில் மற்றும் சதாழில் முயற்ெியாண்மம அபிவிருத்திப் பிரதி அமமச்ெர்) (The Hon. Chathuranga Abeysinghe - Deputy Minister of Industry and Entrepreneurship Development) Hon. Deputy Chairperson of Committees, it was very interesting to listen to my Friend from the Opposition, who spoke and said that we should speak in English so that the World Bank and the IMF would understand and that we are trying to fool the Sri Lankan people by talking in Sinhala. It is an interesting statement. Whether you speak in English or Sinhala or whatever happened in this country, the IMF clearly said that the bankruptcy was due to structural and institutional corruption in this country. The World Bank is also very clearly aware of the level of corruption and mismanagement this country went through, leading it to where we are today. The Leader of the Opposition also spoke about poverty and the problems we have today, which are due to mismanagement of the economy by the so-called experts who could speak in English. That was the problem in this country.

And, unfortunately, my Friend keeps asking us, “What are we going to do with the bonds?” My Friend, the theory and practical application of bonds are also different. You want the Government to say whether it is going to buy or issue bonds, which is market-sensitive information. He is asking the Government to pre-inform the market about what the Government is going to do with the bonds. That is sensitive information, sensitive data and a sensitive decision. Even the book states that it could be done. We have a great plan as to how to manage our debt. Also, we are very confident about our officers in the Ministry, and we are guided by the relevant authorities to ensure that we would manage this crisis far better than they did.

Sir, today, as we speak about this particular Regulation and the change in foreign external outflows, I want to quote one statement, which our Opposition may not have heard, maybe, because it is also in English. It is a statement made at the Seventh Review of the IMF, which states, I quote:

“Sri Lanka’s economy has proved remarkably resilient to successive shocks.

They had clearly stated that Sri Lanka withstood two or three consecutive shocks, but, unfortunately, our Opposition is not willing to understand that. We came out of an economic crisis; we faced Cyclone "Ditwah"; we face a global economic crisis, triggering a fuel crisis and we were also threatened by the tariff changes of the USA. So, that is what the world has understood about this country, and they are also very confident that we could manage this debt, we could repay it and our foreign exchange reserves are good enough for us to move forward.

The Opposition keeps on talking about the gross reserves. The IMF clearly says that their target and condition are based on the net reserves. So, we have, consecutively for the fourth year, achieved a net reserve accumulation of more than US Dollars 1.5 billion. That is very clear and we will be closing the year with reserves amounting to US Dollars 8 billion.

At the same time, we have strengthened our fiscal discipline and have consolidated our revenues, which these experts could not do; the experts who could speak in English could not do so for 76 years. That is the reality in this country. They could not consolidate the revenue from 2015 to 2019; they could not manage their expenses and there was never a time or a Budget, through which they could achieve the revenue target. There was never a Budget through which they could stay within the cost they planned in the first year. And, they are the people who governed this country with overspending and inefficiency in revenue collection. Now, they say that we got a higher revenue because of vehicle imports, and that same person stated in this Parliament last year that that is a tactic at which the Government is going to fail and that it is not going to get much revenue from vehicle imports. Now, the tables have turned and they are saying that that is the only reason why we have revenue consolidation. Obviously, not. We climbed from 13.5 per cent to 16.7

93 94

පාර්ලිදේන්තුෙ

per cent, out of which the additional increase in revenue from vehicle imports was about 1.2 per cent. That is going to continue into this year is what the statistics are showing because people are still importing vehicles. That is what the Fiscal Policy Department has been doing to succeed in all this over the last two-and-a-half years.

What we are doing now is contrary to what the Opposition expected would happen, fearmongering and chasing away the investors. That is exactly what they are doing, fearmongering and trying to predict a bad picture about this country. But, the action we are taking today is giving confidence to the investors. What we are doing is opening up the curtailed or restricted Capital Account of this country; we have been gradually opening it up over the last two years. Sometimes, they refer to Ranil Wickremesinghe as having done a marvelous job in those two years. No, what he did was contract the economy; that was the result. What he did was increase the tax rates, stop imports and restrict the Capital Account. So, if any country would do that, it could preserve foreign exchange. But, we are not doing that. We have opened the entire import market; now, we are opening the capital market and there is no restriction on capital outflows. And, to reach the levels we had earlier, yes, it will take time.

But, the signal we are giving is that our companies here can now invest abroad, without prior approval of the Central Bank, up to US Dollars 750,000 to meet their investment needs. Even a non-resident who is considered a migrant could repatriate about US Dollars 100,000, the threshold of which was US Dollars 200,000 earlier. So, with approval, we are allowing people to take money out of this country. That is the change which is going to take place, and we have come out of the economic crisis in a very smooth way. If you really look at the review before the last under the IMF Programme, you would understand that they would have warned us, if such a threat existed. But, no such threat existed.

At the same time, I have to say that bond issuance or the ISB requirement of this country would be determined by the Government, especially by the Public Debt Management Office of the Ministry of Finance. We do not have to tell the investors or the bondholders what we are going to do. To do that would be ridiculous! So, probably, these are the agents who have been dealing with external ISBs and crippling this country by taking ISBs at an extraordinary interest of 7 per cent in 2015 and 2016. Now, we already know that it was that same Government which existed from 2016 to 2019 which brought in the Foreign Exchange Act and allowed illegal remittances from this country. They have to be accountable for that. They deregulated foreign exchange and ensured that it is not a criminal activity to remit it. Therefore, people used that loophole and sent money out of this country. Now, we are curtailing that. So, those are things the people who could speak English could not do for this country, and we are making that reference now.

Today, we are also seeking approval for an ADB loan to be disbursed to the SME sector of this country. සුළු හා මධයය පත්මාණ ෙයාපාත්කයන්ට තෙත් රුළියලීම බිියන 13ක දේ අවුරුේ අග දී ලැදෙනො. දමාක , අළි දේ අවුරුේ පටන් ගනිේදී ලංකා ිතිහාසදේ ෙැිම ණය ප්‍රමාණය දලස සුළු හා මධයය පත්මාණ ෙයාපාත්කයන්ට රුළියලීම බිියන 5ක ලො දුන්නා. ඒදකන් බිියන 82ක දේ ෙනදකාට ිතාම සාර්ලථකෙ දෙදී ගි දන් තිදෙනො. ඒදකන් සියයට 99ක utilize කර තිදෙනො. ිතිත් ටිකත් දේ කාර්ලතුද දී යයි කියන විශනොසය අපට තිදෙනො. ඊට අමතරෙ තමයි ආසියානු සංෙර්ලධයන ෙැංකුදෙන් ලො ද න රුළියලීම බිියන 13ක ණය අළි දේ පාර්ලිදේන්තුද දී අනුමත කර එබඳයට ද න්දන්. දේදකන් රුළියලීම මිියන 95ක කො ණය ගන්න ඉළුෙන්. අවුරුදු 10ක කො දගෙන්න ඉළුෙන්. අවුරුදු 2ක grace period එකක ලැදෙනො. Export පැත්තට, කාන්තා ෙයෙසායකයන්ට, startupsෙලට - දමාක , startupsෙලට දහාඳ ණය ක්‍රමයක තිබුදණ්ඩ නැහැ - agricultural cropsෙලට, agricultural livestock සඳහා දේ loan එක ලැදෙනො. අප හැම තිසනදසනම අදේ ෙයාපාත්කයන්ට කියන්දන්, සුදුසු ෙයාපෘති ොර්ලතාෙක, දහාඳ CRIB record එකක, ෙැංකුෙත් එකක දහාඳ සහසේෙන්ධයතාෙක තිදෙනො නේ දේ ණය ලො ගන්න ඉළුෙන් කියලායි.

දේ කාරණයත් මම කියන්න ඕනෑ. අප මීට කින්, දේ අවුරුේදේ දී ඉ ණය දකාච්්ර සාර්ලථකෙ දෙදී ගි දන් තිදෙනො කි දොත්, දේ රදේ ජනතාෙ - ෙයෙසායකයන් - 59,000ක ද නා ප්‍රතිලාභ ලො තිදෙනො. ඒදකන්, අදේ අමාතයාංශය ුද ලීම අමාතයාංශය එකක ඍජුෙ ක.මනාකරණය ක.ා, බිියන 25ක. ඒක අදගෝසනතු ෙනදකාට සේූර්ලණදයන් ලොදගන ිෙරයි. දම දදී category 3ක තිදෙනො, micro, small සහ medium යනුදෙන්. Micro කියන්දන්, මිියන 20ට අු. Small කියන්දන්, මිියන 20ත්, 350ත් අතර. මිියන 350ත්, බිියනයත් අතර ප්‍රමාණයක යනකලීම ෙයාපාරෙලට දේ ණය ගන්න ඉළුෙන්. අදේ සාමානය මිනිසනසු දතනො, බිියනයක turnover එකක තිදෙනො නේ එයා හත් දලාකු එකදකදනක දන් කියලා. එදහම නැහැ. ලංකාද තිදෙන ෙයාපාර සනෙභාෙය අනුෙ බිියනය තිදෙන දකදනක කියන්දන්ත් සුළු හා මධයය පත්මාණ ෙයාපාත්කදයක. එදහම වුණත්, අදේ දේ ණයෙින් බිියන 5.9ක ගි දලීමලා තිදෙනො, කුද්‍ර ෙයාපාත්කයන් 1,264ද දනකු දෙත. බිියන 16ක ගි දලීමලා තිදෙනො, සුළු පත්මාණ ෙයාපාත්කයන් 1,13 ද දනකු දෙත. මධයය පත්මාණ ෙයාපාත්කයන් 166ද දනකු පමණයි ණය අරදගන තිදෙන්දන්. ඒදගාලීමලන්ට ගි දන් තිදෙන්දන් බිියන 25න් බිියන 3.5යි. එතදකාට, සියයට 82කම ගි දන් තිදෙන්දන් කුද්‍ර දහෝ සුළු පත්මාණ ෙයාපාත්කයන්ට. ඒ නිසා අළි ඒ තර්ලකයත් බිඳිනො. අළි ඒක දහාඳට monitor කරනො.

ගරු නිදයෝජය කාරක සභා සභාපතිතුමියනි, විපක දේ දමතුමන්ලා තැනින් තැන ිඳදගන දේ ආර්ලිකය ගැන කථා කරනො. සමහර දෙලාෙට ඔය ටියු න් එදහම කරලා ආ ඉ අය ආර්ලිකය ගැන කියන්න ගි දන් දේ ඇප ර දත ණය දකාදහාම ද න්දන් කියලා කියෙ කියො දටියා. අදේ ආණ්ඩුෙ ැන් ඇප ර දත ණය දීලා තිදෙනො, 2,143ද දනකුට. ඇප ර දත ණයක ද නො කියන්දන් දමාකක ? දමච්්ර කලීම වුදණ්ඩ දමාකක ? තමන්ට ශකයතාෙක තිදෙන ෙයාපාරයක තිදෙනො, නුදත් ඇපයක නැහැ. ඇපයක නැති නිසා ෙැංකුෙ ණය ද න්දන් නැහැ.

ගරු නිමයෝගය කාතක සභා සභාපතිුමිය (மாண்புமிகு குழுக்களின் பிரதித் தவிொளர் அவர்கள்) (The Hon. Deputy Chairperson of Committees) ගරු නිදයෝජය ඇමතිතුමනි, ඔෙතුමාට නියමිත කාලය අෙසන්.
